## 13 October 2026 — Motion fidelity correction verified and installed

Root cause: the previous default applied different vertical gains to each directional movement, distorting speed comparisons. The actual first 95 BPM pair has a 40 ms peak-rate ratio of 1.3715; the old emphasis reduced its apparent slope ratio to about 1.126. All 15,341 measured vertices retain raw CC6 times and integrated relative positions (maximum error 1.11e-16). The renderer uses measured line segments, not a generated sine wave. The return starts gently, then accelerates; the recording begins after movement has started, so complete physical onset and polarity are not established.

Implemented in ReferenceAuthoringView.swift and ReferenceAuthoringViewModelTests.swift: Recorded travel is the default and resets on take change; one common affine scale preserves cross-movement slopes.
